H86: DOES THE FULL BARRIER WIN BY SUPPRESSING THE ISSUE RATE.
Apple M4. Run 2026-11-22 21:41 IST. Thresholds fixed before this data
existed, in the commit named below, which also introduced the issueprobe
mode. Counters FIXED_CYCLES, SCHEDULE_UOP_ANY, ARM_STALL_BACKEND and
RETIRE_UOP, each selected by reading its description in
results/kperf-events.txt. SCHEDULE_UOP_ANY counts cycles in which the
scheduler issued at least one uop to any execution unit, so divided by
cycles it is the fraction of time the core made forward progress at all.
The throttling account predicts that fraction is lower for the barrier
arm. See the H86 row in docs/experiments.md.
